#### C-E4 — Receipt operationalization: perf budget, spec-normative canonicalization, conformance in CI

*Обоснование:* The roadmap's own top-risk table (lines 482-493) flags three unmitigated risks even after implementation: no p99 perf budget for receipt emission ('otherwise the receipt gets flag-disabled and the moat evaporates in practice'), canonicalization ambiguity forking DB↔verifier bytes, and conformance/absorption proof rotting outside CI. These are the cheapest insurance on the whole track.

No multi-writer claims anywhere.

### A.4 Track D — Production readiness (single-node -> deployable product)

**Цель:** Make CortexDB honestly deployable in two machine-verified stages. Stage 1: a production-ready SINGLE-NODE v1.0 — crash-safe advisory locking, bounded disk (auto-GC), PITR-by-default, persisted RBAC + verified external identity, p99 latency budgets, structured observability, deployment pack, and an on-disk format freeze — all aggregated into a green `make production-v1-check` that flips PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY from 'not recommended for production' to 'production-ready single-node'. Stage 2: promote Raft from mechanism-without-driver to a running multi-process cluster (election timer, heartbeat loop, persistent hard state, accept loop, cluster ops CLI, measured failover SLOs) while keeping every HA claim behind 'experimental/guarded' until the project's own F02 unfreeze criteria and N-consecutive-green consensus-release-lane evidence hold. All work lands in the repo's gate culture: every task ships a `make <gate>` writing target/<gate>/report.json, and no public claim changes without `make public-claims-check` staying green.

**Ожидаемый эффект:** Measurable: (1) unlocks the first machine-permitted 'production-ready single-node' public claim via production-v1-check (currently forbidden by public-claims-check); (2) zero manual lock interventions across the SIGKILL chaos matrix (today: 1 per kill); (3) disk high-water bounded to <=2x live bytes in 24h soak with retired bytes auto-reclaimed (today: unbounded until manual gc-retired); (4) default-install RPO improves from last-backup to any-committed-seq (PITR on by default); (5) auth upgraded from static bearer tokens to role-based v2 store + signature-verified OIDC with fail-closed JWKS (0 unverified-claim acceptances in negative fixtures); (6) first self-driving Raft cluster: election <2s, kill-leader failover p99 <=5s over >=20 measured trials, 0 lost acked writes — converting consensus-failover-slo-check from 'evidence present/experimental' to 'measured'; (7) HTTP p99 budgets enforced release-over-release (today only mean + queue p95); (8) 100% of persisted formats frozen in a cross-checked manifest with migration + downgrade-refusal fixtures — simultaneously satisfying the project's own F02 'formats stable' unfreeze precondition; (9) tiered storage 10M-cell cold-read evidence packet with zstd compression ratio and RSS bounds recorded.

**Текущее состояние:** Repo root: /mnt/hf_model_weights/arman/3bit/sites/CortexDB (all paths below relative to it). (1) LOCKING: DatabaseLock uses O_EXCL `OpenOptions::create_new` (crates/cortex-engine/src/lock.rs:20) with pid/timestamp metadata; Drop removes the file; after SIGKILL the lock is stale and `StaleLockPolicy::Break` (crates/cortex-engine/src/options.rs:45, applied in crates/cortex-engine/src/database/open.rs:86-91) blindly deletes it — it cannot distinguish a dead holder from a live one; operators fall back to `cortexdb unlock --force` (crates/cortex-cli/src/cli_ops/maintenance.rs:18-24). No libc/rustix/fs2 dependency exists in the workspace. (2) RAFT: complete passive state machines — ElectionState (crates/cortex-engine/src/replication/election.rs, in-memory `voted_for`), ConsensusState (replication/consensus.rs), joint-consensus membership, log matching, snapshot install, TCP transport with token auth (replication/tcp.rs). But there is NO driver: ReplicationPeerServer::serve_n(requests) serves a fixed request count on a blocking listener (replication/peer.rs:46-70), ReplicationNodeRuntime is passive state with no threads (replication/runtime.rs:12-24), no election timer or heartbeat loop exists anywhere in src/, and `voted_for`/`current_term` are NOT durably persisted (ReplicationLog::recover derives current_term as max entry term, replication/log.rs — a Raft safety gap: a restarted node can double-vote in one term). cortex-server has only HTTP ingress forwarding to a leader plus a cached monitor loop (crates/cortex-server/src/cluster.rs, handler.rs:232-306, lifecycle.rs) — all raft-ingress-* gates drive state machines from test harnesses. `consensus-failover-slo-check` (mk/core-security-ops.mk:222) explicitly 'keeps the SLO status experimental until multi-process failover timings are collected' (docs/FUTURE_NON_GOAL_EPICS.md Epic 1). F02/F03 are frozen with explicit unfreeze criteria (docs/DATABASE_GRADE_EXECUTION_PLAN.md:2213-2223). (3) CRYPTO: Track C delivered it — crates/cortex-crypto (argon2, blake3, chacha20poly1305, ed25519-dalek, hmac, sha2, zeroize), AEAD backup v2 with legacy-refuse, keyed audit chain v2, key rotation, Ed25519 receipts (docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md phases 2/6 done). Track D consumes, does not rebuild. (4) IAM: persisted JSON policy store with principals/capabilities/quotas/admin mutation routes + rollback exists (crates/cortex-server/src/auth/{policy,types,parser,routes}.rs, `rbac-policy-store-check`), but roles are flat capability lists (Epic 3 open item: richer role objects); external identity is claims-only — `verify_oidc_claims` takes pre-parsed claims, no JWT signature verification, no live JWKS fetch (crates/cortex-server/src/external_identity.rs:64, external_identity/provider.rs validates config shape only); no token introspection endpoint. (5) PERFORMANCE/STORAGE: payload cache (64MB default, options.rs:104; crates/cortex-engine/src/database/payload_cache.rs); tiered storage v2 flag-gated with `TieredStorageCompressionPolicy::ZstdReserved` placeholder (options.rs:57-65, docs/TIERED_STORAGE_V2.md non-goals = zstd, format versioning, dual-read, prefetch hook); background incremental compaction runs in the server (crates/cortex-server/src/lifecycle.rs) but retired-segment GC is MANUAL only (`Database::garbage_collect_retired_segments`, crates/cortex-engine/src/bundle.rs:79; CLI `gc-retired`) so compacted disk space is never reclaimed automatically; perf gates track mean latency + queue-wait p95 (crates/cortex-server/src/http_metrics/response.rs) — ANN gates have p99 tolerances (mk/ann.mk) but the HTTP serving path has no p99 budget. (6) BACKUP/PITR: `restore_from_backup_to_seq` PITR works (crates/cortex-engine/src/backup/pitr.rs, cli_ops/backup.rs:61-73) but `CORTEXDB_WAL_ARCHIVE` defaults FALSE (crates/cortex-engine/src/config.rs:64), so point-in-time recovery is opt-in; backup-drill/offsite/rpo-rto gates exist. (7) OBSERVABILITY: tracing spans with request_id (handler.rs:59), Prometheus /v1/metrics with actor-queue gauges/histogram (http_metrics/response.rs), background TTL expiry + compaction tasks (lifecycle.rs); no JSON log mode, no consensus metrics. (8) MIGRATION: migration-policy/migration-compatibility/storage-compat gates exist (mk/core-contracts.mk:49-74) but no v1.0 format-freeze manifest enumerating the seven magics (ACLOGv0/ACS3/ACB1/ACI4/ACV1/ACH0/ACM0) plus policy-store/audit-v2/backup-v2/replication-log formats. (9) CLAIMS: docs/PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY.md forbids 'production-ready'; `production-v1-check`/`production-hardening-check`/`production-candidate-check` targets exist in mk/phony.mk as an aggregation lane; deployment pack (systemd unit, launchd plist, nginx) exists at docs/deployment/.

**Целевое состояние:** Stage 1 (single-node v1.0): kernel-enforced advisory flock lock that self-releases on SIGKILL (unlock --force retained only for network filesystems); retired segments auto-GC'd after background compaction under a retention policy, with disk-bound proof in storage soak; WAL archiving on by default with retention tied to backup boundary so `restore --to-seq` works on any default install; a `production` config profile that refuses fail-open combinations at startup; RBAC v2 with named roles/deny rules + `GET /v1/auth/whoami` introspection; external identity with real JWT signature verification (RS256/ES256 via cortex-crypto additions) against pinned-then-live JWKS with TTL cache, fail-closed; tiered storage v2 promoted (zstd page blocks, versioned format, dual-read, executor prefetch hook) with 10M cold-read evidence; HTTP p95/p99 budgets in performance-trend history that fail releases on regression; JSON structured logs, metrics coverage for WAL/backup/compaction/GC/cache; deployment pack v1 verified by gate; machine-readable FORMAT_FREEZE_V1 manifest cross-checked against code magics with migration fixtures + downgrade-refusal tests for every listed format; `make production-v1-check` aggregates all of it and PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY gains an allowed 'production-ready single-node' claim gated on that report. Stage 2 (HA, claims stay experimental until F02 unfreeze + release-lane evidence): durable Raft hard state (term/vote, atomic temp+fsync+rename+CRC32C per storage convention); a RaftDriver with randomized election timer, leader heartbeat/AppendEntries loop, and serve-forever accept loop with clean shutdown; cortex-server write path routed through leader commit behind `experimental_replication`; `cortexdb cluster status|join|remove-node|transfer-leadership` CLI; measured multi-process failover SLO (p99 <= 5s over >= 20 kill-the-leader trials) upgrading consensus-failover-slo-check from 'evidence present' to 'measured'; Prometheus consensus metrics (role/term/commit-applied index/follower lag/election count); Track C's receipt cluster-invariance gates stay green under the real driver.

#### D1 — Single-node operational hardening (lock, GC, PITR defaults, production profile)

*Обоснование:* These are the four concrete gaps between 'beta that passes gates' and 'safe to run unattended': a lock that survives SIGKILL wrongly, disk that only shrinks manually, PITR that is off by default, and no guardrail against fail-open configurations. All are unblocked today and independent of consensus work.

#### D2 — IAM productization: RBAC v2 roles + verified external identity (FUTURE_NON_GOAL order: Epic 3 then Epic 6)

*Обоснование:* The policy store exists but roles are flat capability lists, there is no token introspection, and OIDC support validates claims it never cryptographically verifies. Production IAM = named roles, whoami, real JWT signature verification, live JWKS — reusing Track C's cortex-crypto instead of new crypto decisions.

Deny rules win over allows. Implement v1->v2 store migration on load (follow the existing v0->v1 migration pattern in parser.rs) plus refuse-to-read v3+. Admin mutation routes gain POST /v1/admin/auth/role/{create,update,delete} with the same rollback mechanism as existing policy mutations, audit-chained. #### D3 — Raft runtime driver + cluster ops: mechanism -> self-driving cluster (claims stay experimental per F02 freeze)

*Обоснование:* Every Raft ingredient exists as a hand-stepped state machine; nothing ticks. This epic adds the missing driver (persisted hard state, election timer, heartbeat loop, accept loop), wires it into the server write path behind the existing experimental_replication flag, adds operator commands, and converts the failover SLO gate from 'evidence present' to 'measured'. CLAIM BOUNDARY: all reports carry production_ready=false; public promotion of HA requires the tracker's F02 unfreeze criteria (Level 3, >=3 external HA requests, formats stable >=2 releases — which D6.1 helps satisfy) plus consensus-release-lane-check N consecutive green. Track C's phase-9 receipt gates (receipt-replica-invariance-check, consensus-failover-binder-check, http-raft-routing-accountability-check) must remain green throughout — they are the dependency, not this track's work.

ElectionState mutation points that change term or voted_for (start_election election.rs:81, handle_vote_request:95-112, step_down:148) must persist BEFORE returning the response/request (Raft safety: never emit a vote or a higher-term message that could be lost on crash). Existing replication_election/replication_log test suites green. | `raft-hardstate-check` | M | — |

Driver exposes: start(runtime: ReplicationNodeRuntime, transport, on_commit: Box<dyn Fn(&[ReplicatedEntry])>), status() -> RaftStatus {role, term, leader, commit_index, applied_index, peer_lag}, propose(payload) -> Result<LogIndex> (leader only), shutdown() joining all threads. All timer decisions are driver-side; the pure state machines stay untouched so all existing state-machine tests remain valid. | New multi-threaded (in-process, 3 driver instances with real TCP on 127.0.0.1 ephemeral ports) test: cluster elects exactly one leader within 2s with zero manual stepping; shutting the leader's driver down triggers re-election within 2x election_timeout_max; propose() on the leader reaches commit_index on all 3 nodes; no thread leaks (shutdown joins). #### D4 — Performance program: tiered storage promotion, cache efficiency, p99 budgets

*Обоснование:* The product must hold latency and disk budgets at scale before any production claim. Tiered storage v2's own doc enumerates exactly what promotion requires (zstd, format versioning, dual-read, prefetch); the HTTP path lacks p99 governance that ANN gates already have.

*Обоснование:* Format stability is both the single-node v1.0 promise and the project's own precondition for F02 unfreeze ('formats stable >= 2 releases'). The final task makes the production claim itself a machine-checked artifact, per gate culture.

**Критерии выхода трека:**
- make production-v1-check green with production_single_node_ready=true, all constituent gate reports fresh and hash-recorded under target/production-v1/report.json, and public-claims-check green with the new conditional 'production-ready single-node' claim active.
- Lock liveness: chaos-restart-check SIGKILL matrix completes with zero `unlock --force` invocations; concurrent-open protection proven (second process rejected while first alive).
- Disk bounded: 24h storage-soak evidence shows retired_segment_bytes returning to <1% of live bytes within one GC interval and total disk high-water <= 2x live bytes.
- PITR by default: default-env (no CORTEXDB_WAL_ARCHIVE override) restore --to-seq drill green in pitr-defaults-check.
- IAM: RBAC v2 store round-trip + deny precedence + v1 migration green; whoami contract tests green; JWT signature verification negative fixtures (alg=none, alg confusion, tampered, expired, unknown kid) all rejected; live-JWKS rotation and IdP-down fail-closed proven.
- Raft driver: 3-process cluster self-elects in <2s with zero harness stepping (raft-driver-check, 20 consecutive runs); cluster-write-replication-check green including SIGKILL-leader recovery; consensus-failover-slo-check reports measured=true with p99 <= 5000ms and lost_writes=0 over >= 20 trials; all Track C replication-touching receipt gates still green; consensus-release-lane-check accumulating measured runs.
- Cluster operability: cluster status/join/remove-node/transfer-leadership round-trip green against real processes; consensus Prometheus metrics present on cluster nodes and absent single-node.
- Performance: performance-trend-check history carries per-class p99 with committed budgets and fails on >20% regression; payload-cache hit ratio >= 0.8 on repeat workload; tiered zstd round-trip + dual-read + corruption fail-closed green; 10M cold-read packet recorded.
- Format freeze: format-freeze-check green with 100% magic cross-check; migration-compatibility-check covers every FORMAT_FREEZE_V1 entry including downgrade refusal without panics.
- Honesty invariant held throughout: at no commit does any public doc claim exceed its gate evidence — public-claims-check green at every task boundary, HA claims carry the experimental qualifier until the D3.7 rule's evidence pair exists.

**Зависимости от других треков:** Track C (accountability/crypto) — CONSUMED, NOT DUPLICATED: cortex-crypto crate (AEAD, Ed25519, HMAC, Argon2id, BLAKE3), AEAD backup v2, keyed audit chain v2, key rotation, canonical bytes/determinism, and correctness-prerequisites-check are already done per docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; D2.3 extends cortex-crypto with rsa/p256 under Track C's crypto-deps policy gate; D6.3 requires Track C's crypto-foundation-check green as an input.; Track C phase 9 (transparency log + cluster accountability) — its receipt-replica-invariance-check, consensus-failover-binder-check, http-raft-routing-accountability-check, and multi-agent-cluster-consistency-check are acceptance INPUTS to D3.3: the Raft driver must keep them green; the transparency log and receipt production-readiness blockers remain Track C property.; Web console track — consumes D2.2 (/v1/auth/whoami) and D5.2 metrics families; owns all dashboard UI including the RBAC admin views (FUTURE_NON_GOAL Epic 3 open item 'dashboard admin views'), SLO dashboard rendering, and the dashboard auth-session fix; Track D deliberately builds zero UI.; Retrieval-quality/benchmark track — shares scripts/performance_trend_check.py infrastructure (D4.4 must not break its report schema); no other coupling.; Project freeze policy (not a track): F02/F03 unfreeze criteria in docs/DATABASE_GRADE_EXECUTION_PLAN.md:2213-2223 gate D3 CLAIMS (encoded machine-checkably in D3.7); D6.1's format freeze is itself one of the F02 unfreeze preconditions ('on-disk formats stable >= 2 releases').

### A.5 Track E — Interfaces & ecosystem (продукт вокруг ядра)

**Цель:** Make the Governed Context Engine result type (context_pack.v1 + accountability_receipt.v1) adoptable by external agents, developers, and operators: expand MCP from 3 tools to a full governed-tool surface with resources and streaming; turn the dashboard from an endpoint exerciser into a product console that renders the GCE differentiators (receipts, access decisions, conflicts, memory lifecycle); bring all three SDKs to typed-receipt parity with async + offline receipt verification; publish GCE_CONTRACT v1.0 plus the accountability benchmark against ONE live pgvector+policy baseline; ship real LangChain/LlamaIndex/agent-SDK adapters; and make the 10-minute path to a first verified receipt a machine-checked gate.

**Ожидаемый эффект:** Measurable adoption-surface deltas: MCP tools 3 -> 8 plus >=4 resources and 2 transports (stdio + Streamable HTTP), each covered by a functional gate (today: 0 MCP gates). Dashboard: usable in a browser with auth enabled (today GET /dashboard = 403; target 200 + correct role from whoami); endpoint coverage 19 -> >=30 of ~40 /v1 routes; receipt visibility 0% -> 100% of verify/context responses render signed/absent/failed state with client-side signature verification; 10 grep-marker gates -> functional Playwright gates + pixel-diff visual regression. SDKs: receipt typed in 3/3 SDKs (today 0/3), offline receipt verification available in 4 runtimes (Rust bin, WASM, Python, TS; today 1), parity matrix 100% on required methods (today: no matrix), async support 3/3 languages (today 2/3, Python missing). Spec/benchmark: GCE contract citable by version+requirement-id; 1 live pgvector+policy baseline with a published 6-axis report (today 0 live baselines, static fixture only); installable verification artifacts 0 -> 3 (crates.io + linux/macos binaries). Ecosystem: 2 published framework packages + agent toolkits in both scripting SDKs; time-to-first-verified-receipt < 10 minutes enforced by getting-started gate. These directly unblock the COMMUNITY_ROADMAP beta blockers "Product UI beta readiness" and "SDK publication discipline" and convert the accountability moat (phases 0-8, already green) from internal gates into externally consumable product surface.

**Текущее состояние:** The core moat artifacts exist but are invisible at every adoption surface. (1) MCP: crates/cortex-mcp (689 LOC total: tools.rs, server.rs, sdk_executor.rs, protocol.rs) exposes exactly 3 stdio tools — retrieve_context, verify_fact, remember (tools.rs:82-116); docs/MCP.md explicitly scopes it as "the first adoption surface"; no search/feedback/ingest/explain/receipt tools, no MCP resources, no HTTP/streaming transport, and zero MCP make-gates exist in mk/*.mk. (2) Dashboard: 21 dependency-free files in web/dashboard/src, dual-built by scripts/dashboard_build.py and embedded via crates/cortex-server/src/dashboard.rs; the auth catch-22 is still live — dashboard pages/assets are RouteClass::Admin (crates/cortex-server/src/auth/routes.rs:38-39) so a browser can never load /dashboard when CORTEXDB_AUTH_TOKENS is set; no /v1/auth/whoami exists (grep confirms); role detection is probe-based (web/dashboard/src/app_access.js:1-30); there is NO reporting_receipt.js even though the server now emits signed accountability_receipt on ContextPack/VERIFY (accountability phases 0-8 done per docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md); /v1/context/trace (router.rs:130), /v1/conflicts, /v1/feedback[/stats] (router.rs:233-235), and /v1/agents admin have zero UI; ~10 dashboard gates in mk/performance-dashboard.mk:84-121 are static grep-marker checks. (3) SDKs: published (Rust crates, PyPI/npm cortexdb-sdk per commits c5a3dbd/ac7d9cd/6fb6a0d) but receipt is untyped everywhere — Rust Option<serde_json::Value> (crates/cortex-sdk/src/types/context.rs:122), Python dict[str,Any] (sdk/python/_cortexdb_client/model_types/verification.py:76), TS Record<string,unknown> (sdk/typescript/cortexdb-client/types/verification.ts:36); Rust has async_client/, Python is sync-only (transport.py has retries), TS has withRetries (client.ts:22); no SDK can verify a receipt offline (only the Rust binary crates/cortex-receipt-verify). (4) Spec: docs/spec/{GCE_CONTRACT.md,GCE_CONFORMANCE.md,ACCOUNTABILITY_RECEIPT_V1.md,RECEIPT_VERIFIER.md} exist with gates gce-spec-doc-check/receipt-threat-model-check/aab-conformance-check (mk/core.mk:36-44), but conformance uses only a static thin-wrapper fixture (fixtures/gce_conformance/thin_wrapper_reference.json); the ONE live pgvector+OPA/Cedar baseline (BASE-1/BASE-2, docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_ROADMAP.md:617-623) is unbuilt (zero pgvector code in scripts/); the spec has no version header, requirement IDs, or third-party conformance template. (5) Integrations: examples/integrations/{langchain_retriever,llm_tool_calling,memory_chat_agent} are mock-LLM demos gated by live-integration-examples-check (mk/core-release-public.mk:84) — no installable langchain/llamaindex packages. (6) Docs/community: book.toml mdBook config exists (src=docs, site-url /CortexDB/), docs-site-check/getting-started-check/community-roadmap-check/contributor-onboarding-check exist; legacy ui/index.html is dead divergence risk; docs/COMMUNITY_ROADMAP.md names "Dashboard product surface" and "SDK publication discipline" as beta blockers.

**Целевое состояние:** MCP adapter v2: 8 governed tools (retrieve_context, verify_fact, remember, search, submit_feedback, ingest_text, explain_context, verify_receipt), MCP resources exposing the frozen schemas and GCE contract, optional Streamable-HTTP transport with progress notifications, all fail-closed through the same server auth/AgentView path, gated by functional stdio+HTTP tests. Dashboard = product console: browser-reachable under auth (session/whoami), receipt badge + client-side WASM verification on every Verify/Context response, ContextPack trace inspector with admitted/denied access decisions, SVG VERIFY conflict graph with typed conflicts, Memory lifecycle route (remember/forget/conflicts/feedback/TTL timeline), AgentView editor with dry-run probes, opt-in telemetry sparklines, and every feature gate converted from grep-marker to functional Playwright with visual regression. SDKs: one parity matrix at 100% across Rust/Python/TS, typed AccountabilityReceipt generated from docs/schemas/accountability_receipt.v1.json, offline receipt verification in Python and TS cross-validated against cortex-receipt-verify, Python async client, uniform retry/idempotency ergonomics. Spec & benchmark: GCE_CONTRACT v1.0 with RFC-2119 requirement IDs, changelog, and third-party conformance statement template published on the mdBook site; ONE live pgvector+policy baseline running the Track C v0.5 axis harness (scope-leak@budget, citation P/R, contradiction recall, tokens-to-answer, receipt-verifiability, determinism) with the baseline UNRANKED on receipt-verifiability/determinism and CortexDB RANKED on all six; cortex-receipt-verify installable from crates.io + release binaries. Ecosystem: pip-installable langchain-cortexdb and llamaindex-cortexdb, agent-toolkit helpers inside the SDKs, pinned-version integration CI matrix, a sub-10-minute machine-checked quickstart ending at a verified receipt, and a community RFC process for spec evolution.

Every new tool must route through the Rust SDK so server-side binder/AgentView fail-closed algebra governs it (no client-side policy).

#### TE2 — Dashboard → product web console (receipts, trace, conflicts, memory, agents)

*Обоснование:* The console is an endpoint exerciser that hides every GCE differentiator: signed receipts are ignored, /v1/context/trace and /v1/conflicts have no UI, the auth catch-22 makes the page unreachable with auth on, and ~10 feature gates are grep checks. This epic turns it into the product surface for the accountability contract while keeping the dependency-free constraint.

#### TE3 — SDK parity + ergonomics (typed receipts, offline verification, async, retries)

*Обоснование:* All three published SDKs treat the moat artifact as an untyped blob (Rust serde_json::Value at types/context.rs:122, Python dict, TS Record) and none can verify a receipt offline; Python lacks async; retry semantics differ per language. Adoption of the result contract requires the receipt to be a first-class typed, verifiable object in every client.

#### TE4 — Open GCE contract v1.0 + accountability benchmark publication

*Обоснование:* docs/spec/GCE_CONTRACT.md exists and aab-conformance-check proves the category boundary against a static thin-wrapper fixture, but the spec is unversioned, has no third-party conformance path, and the ONE live pgvector+policy baseline (BASE-1/BASE-2, ACCOUNTABILITY_ROADMAP.md:617-623) is unbuilt. Publication converts internal gates into an externally citable category contract — the anti-absorption move.

| ID | Задача | Файлы | Что сделать | Приёмка | Гейт | Усилие | Зависит |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Create CONFORMANCE_STATEMENT_TEMPLATE.md: the checklist a third-party implementation fills per requirement ID with evidence pointers, mapping each ID to the aab-conformance axes. | `TE4.2` | **ONE live pgvector+policy baseline (BASE-1/BASE-2) under the six accountability axes** | baselines/pgvector_policy/ (new: docker-compose.yml with pinned postgres+pgvector and OPA image digests, thin_wrapper_runner.py, policies/*.rego, README.md); fixtures/gce_conformance/thin_wrapper_reference.json (keep as the documented static reference); scripts/aab_conformance_check.py (read for report format); docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_ROADMAP.md:617-623 (BASE-1/BASE-2 definition); mk/core.mk (new nightly target) | Implement the one honest live baseline the roadmap demands: a documented thin wrapper of pgvector (dense + tsvector lexical) + OPA policy filtering + application-side context assembly, ingesting the same corpus/fixture set the Track C v0.5 axis harness uses. Run the Track C harness against it for all six axes: scope-leak@budget, citation precision/recall, contradiction recall, tokens-to-answer, receipt-verifiability, determinism. The baseline is structurally UNRANKED on receipt-verifiability (it cannot emit a plan-bound signed receipt) and determinism (no canonical-bytes contract) — record UNRANKED honestly, do not score 0. - make mcp-adapter-v2-check green in the release lane: 8 tools + 4 resources + stdio and Streamable-HTTP transports, with the fail-closed out-of-scope test passing on both transports and the golden stdio transcript unchanged for the original 3 tools.

- Zero grep-marker dashboard feature gates remain: context-pack-explorer-check, verification-explorer-check, permissions-view-check, audit-viewer-v2-check, backup-restore-view-check, incident-view-check, dashboard-role-ui-check, dashboard-operational-status-check, single-node-slo-dashboard-check are all functional Playwright checks, plus dashboard-visual-regression-check with committed goldens; legacy ui/index.html deleted.

- gce-spec-v1-check green: GCE_CONTRACT.md carries version 1.0, unique GCE-R requirement IDs, changelog, and a third-party conformance statement template; docs-site-check builds all spec pages.
- aab-baseline-matrix-report green and reproducible: ONE live pgvector+OPA baseline measured on four axes and UNRANKED on receipt-verifiability/determinism, CortexDB RANKED on all six; aab-publication-check proves every published number traces to gate evidence; public-claims-check remains green.
- receipt-verifier-distribution-check green: cortex-receipt-verify installable on a clean machine (crates.io + checksummed binaries) and verifying the golden receipt in under 5 minutes.
- integration-langchain-check, integration-llamaindex-check, integration-agent-tools-check, integrations-matrix-check green with pinned framework versions and fail-closed scope-enforcement tests; packages publication-ready (dry-run) pending release-owner activation.
- getting-started-check extended: the byte-extracted quickstart reaches a verified receipt in <10 minutes on CI; community-roadmap-check green with the new interface milestones and >=10 bounded Track-E starter issues.
- All new gates write target/<gate>/report.json, are wired into alpha-check/release-check per mk/release.mk conventions, and no Track E doc or UI copy claims production receipts, HA, or compliance (receipt-production-ready-check boundary respected).

**Зависимости от других треков:** Track C (verification & accountability benchmark v0.5): owns the axis METRIC HARNESS — scope-leak@budget, citation P/R, contradiction recall, tokens-to-answer, receipt-verifiability, determinism runners and their fixture corpora. TE4.2/TE4.3 only build the live pgvector+OPA baseline stack and the publication packet on top of those runners; if the v0.5 harness slips, TE4.2 is blocked (TE4.1 spec versioning is not).; Track A/B (engine core & accountability P9 continuation): receipt emission, captured admitted/denied access decisions, canonical bytes, transparency anchors are already landed (docs/ACCOUNTABILITY_IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md phases 0-8 done, 9 partial) and are consumed as-is by TE1.3/TE2.2/TE2.3/TE3.x. Production receipt claims remain gated by receipt-production-ready-check owned there — all Track E UI/docs must render the 'local receipt, not production custody' boundary verbatim.; Track owning retrieval quality (embedding auto-ingest, rank-fusion fix): MCP `search` and SDK search expose whatever the engine returns; quality improvements (hybrid-by-default, ingest-time embedding) are NOT Track E scope — TE1.1/TE3.4 must not add client-side reranking to compensate.; Track owning F04 agent transactions: the server-side persisted idempotency ledger. TE3.5 only plumbs the Idempotency-Key header and documents echo-only semantics until that ledger lands.; Track owning server memory-lifecycle semantics: TE2.5 adds a read-only GET /v1/memory/lifecycle interface over crates/cortex-engine/src/memory/lifecycle.rs without changing decay math; any decay-model change there must keep the endpoint's field contract.; Release-owner approval (project governance, per docs/FUTURE_NON_GOAL_EPICS.md promotion rules and PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY): actual PyPI/npm/crates.io publication in TE4.4/TE5.1/TE5.2 ships as dry-run-green until explicitly activated.

### A.6 Track F — Validation & benchmark program (доказательство качества)

**Цель:** Make every quality, performance, and accountability claim CortexDB publishes machine-produced, snapshot-versioned, regression-gated in the correct CI lane, and cross-verified on a public results page. One evidence registry (benchmark_report.v1 snapshots) becomes the single source all tracks report into; fast lane runs only offline-deterministic fixture gates, nightly runs full offline re-runs and snapshot-based competitor scoring, and key-requiring official runs (gpt-5.4 re-judge, LLM readers/judges) live in an explicit manual-evidence lane with honest blocked/ready status per PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY.

**Ожидаемый эффект:** Measurable: (a) ERB headline becomes leaderboard-comparable (judge_model=gpt-5.4 recorded in evidence) or carries an honest machine-recorded blocked status — removing the largest asterisk in docs/STATUS.md:52; (b) any per-type regression >2.0 recall points (LongMemEval types, ERB categories) or >0.02 MultiHop Hits@10 / LoCoMo hit@10 drop is caught nightly instead of at release; (c) first-ever LoCoMo QA end-to-end number and packaged MultiHop 0.72 give CortexDB 4 external benchmarks with committed reproducible evidence; (d) AAB-mini produces the first numeric six-axis accountability score with CortexDB RANKED and every snapshot rival UNRANKED on receipt-verifiability/determinism — the category claim becomes empirical; (e) 100% of published benchmark numbers machine-verified against evidence (results-page-check), replacing grep-marker checks; (f) nightly benchmark lane requires 0 API keys and 0 live competitor calls (flake-free by construction); (g) other tracks (retrieval levers, accountability, dashboard) get a single reporting contract — new baselines enter via registry snapshots, never by editing gate code.

**Текущее состояние:** Strong gate culture but fragmented measurement. (1) ERB: headline 47.74 is interim — erb-submission/README.md and docs/STATUS.md:52 both carry the boundary "not leaderboard-comparable until gpt-5.4 re-judge"; the re-judge command exists only as prose in docs/archive/ENTERPRISE_RAG_BENCH_SUBMISSION.md:166-189 (LLM_MODEL_NAME=gpt-5.4 against frozen erb-submission/answers.jsonl), no make target or gate. (2) LongMemEval: official retrieval gates exist (mk/longmemeval.mk, scripts/longmemeval/check_v1_retrieval_adapter.py) but assert only overall recall_all@10/ndcg_any@10 presence (line 106); per-type analysis exists in scripts/longmemeval/analyze_v1_results.py (by_question_type) yet there is NO committed per-type baseline and NO regression gate — single-session-preference 0.2667 could regress silently. (3) LoCoMo is retrieval-only (mk/external-benchmarks-locomo.mk; hit@1 0.3199 / hit@10 0.6312 in docs/BENCHMARKS.md:360-372), no QA score ever produced. (4) MultiHop-RAG has full official runs (accuracy 0.72, Hits@10 0.9902, docs/BENCHMARKS.md:734-757) but results live only in prose tables — no committed baseline, no gate. (5) Competitor comparison is a static naive-stack matrix (baseline-comparison-check, docs/BASELINE_COMPARISON.md, SQLite FTS5 + hashed vectors); the accountability program already landed the STRUCTURAL conformance suite — aab-conformance-check with fixtures/gce_conformance/thin_wrapper_reference.json, scope-leak-bench-check, verify-conflict-recall-check, accountability-receipt-verify/tamper-check, pack-determinism-hash-check (mk/core.mk:42, scripts/aab_conformance_check.py) — but there is no QUANTIFIED six-axis AAB run (docs/schemas/ has accountability_receipt.v1.json but no aab_run.v1.json) and no competitor snapshots. (6) Performance trends are solid (performance-trend-check, continuous-benchmark-gate/hosted in mk/performance-dashboard.mk:45-48, fixtures/performance/history/, release-regression-dashboard-check) but scripts/release_regression_dashboard.py RULES (lines 22-37) cover storage/search/context/verify/api/sdk only — external benchmark scores have no trend protection. (7) Public numbers are hand-maintained string markers: scripts/public_benchmarks_check.py greps literal values ("session recall_all@10=0.9021") in docs/archive/PUBLIC_BENCHMARKS.md; docs/STATUS.md benchmark table is unverified prose. (8) Lanes are defined in docs/CI_LANES.md and .github/workflows/nightly.yml but no audit binds benchmark gates to lanes.

**Целевое состояние:** A benchmark evidence registry (docs/schemas/benchmark_report.v1.json + fixtures/benchmarks/registry/) holds one immutable snapshot per official run for LongMemEval, ERB-500, LoCoMo, MultiHop-RAG, AAB, and perf, each recording dataset hash, harness commit, exact answerer/judge model IDs, per-type metrics, and boundary flags (leaderboard_comparable, judge_official). The ERB gpt-5.4 re-judge is a one-command target with a ready/blocked gate; a same-judge comparator makes cross-judge deltas impossible to publish. Per-type regression gates (LongMemEval 5 types, ERB question types, MultiHop official scorer, LoCoMo hits) run nightly fully offline against committed baselines, with fixture self-tests in the fast lane. AAB-mini quantifies the six accountability axes (scope-leak@budget, citation P/R, conflict recall/false-conflict, tokens-to-answer, receipt-verifiability, determinism) on a CI-safe fixture in the release lane, and a snapshot-based competitor matrix (pgvector+OPA thin wrapper mandatory, Zep/Mem0 optional captures) re-scores offline nightly — zero live third-party API calls in CI. docs/RESULTS.md is generated from the registry and byte-diff-verified; docs/STATUS.md benchmark rows are cross-checked against registry snapshots; benchmark-trend-check joins performance-trend-check in release-check. A lane audit guarantees every benchmark gate is assigned to exactly one of {fast, nightly, release, manual-evidence} and that fast/nightly gates need no network or keys.

*Обоснование:* The single biggest honesty asterisk is 'not leaderboard-comparable until gpt-5.4 re-judge' (docs/STATUS.md:52). Make the re-judge a one-command, tamper-proof operation over the frozen answers, and make cross-judge score comparisons structurally impossible to publish.

**Критерии выхода трека:**
- benchmark-report-schema-check, benchmark-registry-check, results-page-check, benchmark-trend-check, benchmark-lane-audit-check, erb-run-compare-check green inside alpha-check; aab-mini-check green inside release-check.
- ERB re-judge state is machine-recorded: either target/enterprise-rag-bench/official-rejudge/report.json status=passed with judge_model=gpt-5.4 and a leaderboard_comparable=true registry snapshot (STATUS.md/RESULTS.md boundary flips automatically via the generated page), or an honest status=blocked report — and no cross-judge delta can be published (comparator refusal enforced by gate).
- All four external-benchmark regression gates (LongMemEval per-type, ERB category, MultiHop official-scorer, LoCoMo) green in the nightly lane for 14 consecutive runs with zero red flakes (blocked-status allowed), before any promotion of thresholds.
- AAB: aab_run.v1 spec frozen; CortexDB verdict=RANKED on aab-mini with tamper case flipping to UNRANKED; aab-snapshot-matrix-check green fully offline with >=2 systems and every rival UNRANKED on receipt-verifiability and determinism.
- 100% of benchmark numbers in docs/RESULTS.md and docs/STATUS.md are cross-verified against committed registry snapshots (hand-edit fails CI); registry history is append-only (sha256 index enforced).
- Lane audit proves: every Track F gate assigned to exactly one lane; fast and nightly lanes require zero API keys and zero live third-party network calls.

**Зависимости от других треков:** Accountability/crypto track (already substantially landed in-tree): standalone receipt verifier + accountability_receipt.v1 (accountability-receipt-verify-check, docs/schemas/accountability_receipt.v1.json) and pack-determinism-hash-check are consumed by F4.2 axes 5-6; Ed25519 signing config is consumed by F4.4 pack signing (unsigned packs record signed=false until it lands); scope-leak-bench-check surface enumeration is reused for AAB axis 1; the determinism fix (no wall-clock in VERIFY outputs) must hold for axis 6 byte-identity.; Correctness/VERIFY track: DV7 labeled contradiction corpus and verify-conflict-recall-check label format are reused for AAB axis 3 seeded conflicts (F4.2); conflict unit/currency normalization changes re-baseline via registry snapshots, not gate edits.; Retrieval-quality track (benchmark levers): owns the b8 e2e ERB answer+judge run, hybrid/embedding retrieval, candidate-pool deepening, and the LongMemEval type-aware generation + compact-context runs. Track F provides the scoring/packaging contract they flow through (F2.2 same-judge comparator, F2.3 per-type packaging, F3.1/F3.2 re-baselining via registry snapshots); Track F never modifies retrieval code.; Webapp/dashboard track: the /dashboard/quality evidence route consumes target/benchmark-registry/report.json; Track F freezes that contract (F1.2), the dashboard track builds the UI.; Docs/release infrastructure (existing, no new work): docs-pages.yml mdbook site publishes docs/RESULTS.md; release-check/alpha-check aggregates in mk/release.mk receive the new gates.; External/operational: gpt-5.4 judge API access for F2.1 (gate stays honestly blocked until available); DeepSeek key for LoCoMo QA (F3.4) and any MultiHop QA re-runs; a nightly runner with the cached ERB corpus DB for the full-500 category regression path (blocked-status fallback otherwise).

## Приложение B. Секвенирование и состязательная критика

Два независимых прохода. **B.1** — мастер-секвенсор (5 фаз, 36–44 недели). **B.2** — состязательный критик, чьи поправки ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНЫ для исполнителя: (1) немедленный бэкап незакоммиченного дерева; (2) единые владельцы дублей (preference: B3.1 владеет; temporal windows: одна задача; whoami: D2.2 владеет; pgvector-бейзлайн: Track F); (3) численные бенчмарк-цели — в нарратив, гейты проверяют доказательства+отсутствие регрессий; (4) Track F (реестр доказательств, per-PR eval-луп) — ПЕРЕД ранжировочными задачами Track A; (5) добавить задачу выбора embedding-модели; (6) решить «судью записи» для ERB; (7) latency-бюджет для A7.2.

### B.1 Мастер-секвенсор (5 фаз, v0.2.1 → v1.0)

**Вердикт.** EXECUTABLE, with one non-negotiable ordering constraint: the entire accountability implementation (341 dirty files incl. 148 untracked — cortex-crypto, cortex-receipt-verify, accountability/, ~60 gates) exists ONLY in the uncommitted working tree, so C1-1/C1-2 phase-ordered landing + clean-clone CI is Phase 1 and precedes every other track's engine merge; the master tracker (DATABASE_GRADE_EXECUTION_PLAN.md, 0 accountability mentions) does not know the moat exists. After landing, the plan splits into two spines — the accountability spine (C1→C3 contracts→C4 operationalization→C2 external evidence→D6.3 production claim) and the quality spine (A1 fusion fix→A2 embeddings→A7 engine-path productization→A6.4 honest ERB ≥60) — with Tracks D/E/F running substantially parallel on disjoint files. Two days-scale wins (A6.1 b8 scoring ~62-68 ERB combined; A6.2 type-aware prompts →LongMemEval ≥0.80) land in Phase 1 to establish the honest evidence baseline everything else regresses against via the F1 registry. v1.0 = machine-checked production-ready-single-node (production-v1-check), deliberately DECOUPLED from receipt-production evidence (KMS/HSM, external compliance, ≥2 witnesses — external-dependency items that may honestly stay production_ready=false) and from HA claims (Raft driver ships experimental behind the D3.7 machine rule; F02/F03 claim freeze respected). Estimated 36-44 weeks total for one strong implementation agent with an orchestrator, assuming benchmark runs and soak tests execute unattended in the background. Duplicate ownership must be resolved before Phase 2 (whoami: D2.2 owns, TE2.1 consumes; preference extraction: B3.1 owns, A4.3 reduces to intent detection + pack slot merged with B3.2; temporal windows: A4.1 owns derivation, B2.2 owns the boost constant — one module, one gate each).

**Критический путь:**
1. 1. BACKUP + C1-1: push a safety branch of the 341-file working tree on day one, then land the accountability implementation in phase-ordered compiling commits — every other engine merge is blocked behind this (the moat is one `git checkout .` from deletion)
2. 2. C1-2: clean-clone CI re-verification of all ~60 accountability gates (the local Verification Log is not reproducible evidence) + C1-3 tracker integration
3. 3. C3-1: frozen-weights ranking-change protocol — the mandatory landing mechanism without which no Track A ranking change can merge safely against determinism_hash goldens
4. 4. F1.1→F1.2: evidence registry frozen and seeded — the reporting contract every subsequent benchmark number flows through (with F2.2 same-judge guard before any new ERB scoring)
5. 5. A6.1 + A6.2 (parallel, harness-only): honest headline baselines banked (ERB ~62-68 via b8; LongMemEval ≥0.80 via type-aware prompts) — these define the regression floor for all engine work
6. 6. A1.1 (fusion normalization) → A1.2 (corpus BM25) → A1.3/A1.4: the ranking-correctness gate that A2/A3/A4/A5/A7 and B2.2 all depend on
7. 7. A2.1 → A2.2 (ingest-time auto-embedding): the single biggest transferable capability; unblocks A6.3, A6.4, and the ERB semantic-type loss pool (n=125 at 28.8%)
8. 8. B2.1 (supersession) + B1.3 (idempotency ledger): the memory-correctness substrate for B4/B6 consolidation and transactions
9. 9. A7.1 → A7.2 (two-stage engine retrieve at b8-parity recall) → A4.2/A7.3 (coverage + MMR as plan-visible options, receipt-schema-coordinated)
10. 10. A6.4: the exit-proof ERB engine-path run ≥60/≥75% — proves the product path produces the benchmark ranking (the GCE honesty requirement)
11. 11. F4.1→F4.2 (AAB-mini RANKED) → TE4.1→TE4.2→TE4.3 (GCE contract v1.0 + live pgvector baseline + publication): the category-defining external artifact
12. 12. D6.1→D6.2 (format freeze + migrations, also starts the F02 'stable ≥2 releases' clock) → D6.3 (production-v1-check aggregate) → v1.0 claim flip
13. 13. LONG-LEAD PARALLEL SPUR (start Phase 3, close Phase 5 or hold honestly): C2-1 KMS/HSM custody → C2-2 compliance certification → C2-3 witness quorum → C2-4 production receipt claim

**Параллельные дорожки:**
- Track D single-node hardening (D1 lock/GC/PITR/profile, D2 IAM, D4 perf/tiered, D5 observability) runs fully parallel to Tracks A/B from Phase 2 onward — disjoint files (lock.rs, lifecycle.rs, auth/, storage codecs) vs ranking/search/memory; only touch-points are D2.2 whoami (consumed by TE2.1) and D4.2 prefetch (must prove determinism_hash invariance)
- Track E interfaces (MCP crates/cortex-mcp, dashboard web/, SDKs sdk/) runs fully parallel throughout — consumes frozen C3-2 receipt artifacts and D2.2 whoami, never touches engine ranking; TE4.2 AAB baseline is the only Track E task blocked on another track's harness (F4/C v0.5 axis runners)
- Track F validation program (scripts/benchmarks, fixtures/registry, mk/validation-program.mk) runs fully parallel throughout — it never modifies retrieval code by charter; the only sequencing rule is F3.x baselines commit BEFORE Track A/B harness changes alter rankings
- D3 Raft driver (replication/ modules) is fully parallel to everything — no other track touches those files; its only cross-dependency is keeping Track C's replication-touching receipt gates green (D3.3 acceptance input), and its claims are frozen by D3.7 regardless of code progress
- C2 production-evidence acquisition (KMS/HSM, compliance reviewer, external witnesses) is operator/external work running in the background across Phases 3-5 — zero code contention, pure calendar lead time
- LLM-dependent benchmark runs (GPT-4o generation, gemma/gemini judging, gpt-5.4 re-judge, DeepSeek LoCoMo QA) execute as unattended background jobs orchestrated between coding tasks — they consume wall-clock, not agent time

**Топ-риски:**
| Риск | Митигейт |
|---|---|
| The uncommitted moat (148 untracked + 193 modified files: cortex-crypto, cortex-receipt-verify, all receipt/transparency code) is destroyed or corrupted by a stray reset/checkout before C1-1 completes — the single largest existential risk in the plan. | Hour-zero action before any other work: `git stash create` snapshot + commit the entire tree to a safety branch pushed to remote; then perform the phase-ordered landing on a working branch. Freeze all other tracks' engine merges until C1-2 is green (landing precedence is explicit in Track C). No agent runs any git write operation outside the landing task during Phase 1. |
| Cross-track merge conflicts and determinism breakage: Tracks A/B edit the same cortex-engine ranking/search/verification files Track C just landed, and any un-versioned scoring change silently breaks pack_determinism_hash goldens and signed receipts. | C3-1 frozen-weights protocol is landed in Phase 1 as a hard gate with a red-test (unversioned weight edit fails ranking-weights-drift-check); every ranking PR re-baselines weights-version-binding + pack-determinism goldens in the same change; the orchestrator enforces single-writer ownership per file area (retrieval_rank.rs = Track A only after C1-1; access.rs/allowed.rs = C-owned; harness scripts = one owner per file per phase). |
| Benchmark exit targets miss (ERB engine-path ≥60, LongMemEval ≥0.80, recall_all@10 ≥0.93) after weeks of engine work, or gains are achieved with bench-only crutches that violate the no-overfit rule. | Bank the ceiling proof first (A6.1 b8 scoring shows ~62-68 is reachable with existing components; A6.2 prompts are proven on DeepSeek 0/30→20/30); every 500-question run is preceded by a compact-50 gate per repo rule; F3 per-type regression baselines are committed BEFORE harness changes so slips are visible per-question; A6.4 explicitly requires engine-path recall within 2pts of the bench pipeline, making crutch-transfer the acceptance criterion rather than an afterthought; WeightedScoreReranker defaults stay generic (kill-hardcoded-overfit rule). |
| External dependencies never materialize: gpt-5.4 judge access, real KMS/HSM custody, an external compliance reviewer, and ≥2 independent transparency witnesses are all outside the implementation agent's control — chasing them could stall v1.0 indefinitely. | Every external-input gate is built ready/blocked (exit 0 with named blocker codes, per the existing readiness pattern); v1.0's production-v1-check is deliberately decoupled from receipt-production-ready-check — the honest fallback (production_ready=false, claims capped at 'signed receipt, beta, local trust anchor, not leaderboard-comparable') is a first-class exit state encoded in PUBLIC_CLAIMS_POLICY machine rules, not a failure. C2 acquisition starts in Phase 3 to maximize calendar lead. |
| D3 Raft driver (2x XL tasks) is the highest-complexity, highest-flake work in the plan: multi-process timing tests, thread lifecycle, and a Raft safety gap (unpersisted voted_for) — it could consume weeks and destabilize CI. | D3.1 hard-state persistence lands first (pure safety fix, testable single-process); driver work is isolated in new replication/driver.rs with pure state machines untouched so all existing tests stay valid; gates run 20x to surface flake before lane wiring; the single-node path must be provably zero-cost with the flag off (perf-trend acceptance); D3.7's machine rule means the whole epic can slip past v1.0 without blocking the production claim — it is explicitly not on the critical path. |
| Plan overload for one implementation agent: ~135 tasks across six tracks; attempting everything serially blows the schedule and invites half-landed features that break the gate culture. | Phases have hard milestone exits and the cuts list defers non-load-bearing scope (OCR, 100M claims, Zep/Mem0 captures, i18n, cell-id migration, F03 productization); benchmark/soak jobs run unattended in the background; each task is self-contained with files/acceptance/gate so the orchestrator can re-order within a phase without dependency archaeology; the two-consecutive-green promotion requirements (B6.4) have scheduled slack rather than being afterthoughts. |
| Receipt schema churn: A4.2 coverage and A7.3 diversity add plan-visible options that must appear in signed receipts; uncoordinated field additions would fork the canonicalization the verifier depends on. | A receipt-schema minor-version process is a named pre-condition for A7.3 in Phase 3 (C track owns the schema; Track A exposes fields, never signs); C4-2 cross-language goldens are landed in Phase 3 so any canonicalization divergence is caught as a spec bug; new metadata fields (embedding_ref, TemporalWindow) enter the canonical serialization set at A2.1/A4.1 time with golden re-baselines in the same PR. |

**Срезы и пробелы:**
- CUT (explicit, per project non-goals): OCR/enrichment ingestion pipelines — A8.3 ships adapter conformance contracts only, docs keep saying 'not implemented'; full OCR stays a post-1.0 product track.
- CUT: 100M-scale claims — only the 10M tiered cold-read evidence packet (D4.5) ships; 100M remains explicitly unclaimed.
- CUT: Zep/Mem0/Cognee live competitor captures — the AAB matrix ships with the ONE mandatory pgvector+OPA baseline; cloud-competitor snapshots are optional later additions in the same snapshot format (anti-benchmark-theater rule).
- CUT: multi-writer/load-balanced replication and any production HA claim — Raft ships experimental-only at v1.0 behind the D3.7 machine rule; F02/F03 unfreeze and consensus productization are post-1.0 (D6.1's format freeze starts their precondition clock).
- CUT: agent-cell-id.v2 31-bit widening — C3-4 delivers the ADR decision only; the migration (if GO) is a post-1.0 epic. The 2^28 bound becomes an enforced, documented product limit (B1.2).
- CUT: dashboard i18n (RU/KK UI) despite the multilingual analyzer; EN-only console through v1.0. Preference/temporal extraction patterns are EN-first with RU/KK only where the analyzer token tables make it trivial.
- CUT: F09 managed cloud — untouched, all cloud-prerequisite gates keep managed_cloud_ready=false.
- CUT: MCP OAuth/authorization spec work beyond token passthrough — HTTP transport ships with the existing bearer model; full MCP auth spec compliance deferred.
- GAP RESOLVED BY ASSIGNMENT — whoami double-ownership: D2.2 and TE2.1 both specify GET /v1/auth/whoami. Single owner = D2.2 (server, Phase 2); TE2.1 consumes it for the dashboard. Orchestrator must not schedule both as written.
- GAP RESOLVED BY ASSIGNMENT — preference extraction triple-spec: A4.3 and B3.1 both create memory/preference_extract.rs with overlapping lexicons, and both define preference-extract-check. Single owner = B3.1 (extractor) + B3.2 (pack slot + intent detection, absorbing A4.3's search/intent.rs work). One module, one gate.
- GAP RESOLVED BY ASSIGNMENT — temporal windows double-spec: A4.1 and B2.2 both derive windows from Date anchors and add a frozen Q16 boost. Single implementation: A4.1 owns query_understanding derivation + conditions; B2.2 reduces to the frozen boost constant + validity-interval intersection and the B2.4 harness evidence. Gates merge (temporal-window-check supersedes temporal-window-boost-check or vice versa — pick one name).
- GAP — LongMemEval harness contention: scripts/longmemeval/v1_cortexdb_retrieval.py is modified by A4.1, A6.3, B2.4, B3.3, and B5.3. Serialize through one owner per phase and commit the F3.1 per-type baseline BEFORE the first ranking-affecting harness change, else regression attribution is lost.
- GAP — MMR diversity is specified three ways (A4.4 SearchQuery override, A7.3 API/AQL option, B5.3 harness usage): implement once at the engine layer (A4.4 in Phase 3), then A7.3 exposes it API-level; B5.3 consumes. Do not add a third code path.
- GAP — idempotency semantics honesty: TE3.5 documents Idempotency-Key as echo-only, but B1.3's server ledger lands in Phase 2 and B6.3's API in Phase 4 — the SDK docs and parity matrix must be updated when server enforcement becomes real, or the honesty note goes stale in the wrong direction. Add a doc-flip step to B6.3 acceptance.
- GAP — no task provisions infrastructure: the nightly runner with the cached ERB corpus DB (F3.2), multi-process failover capacity (D3.5), docker for baseline/witness stacks (TE4.2, C2-3), and API-key custody for the manual-evidence lane are operator actions with no owner in any track. Assign to the orchestrator/operator explicitly in Phase 1.
- GAP — receipt schema evolution process: plan-visible retrieve options (A4.2 coverage, A7.3 diversity, B2.1 latest_only) all touch the signed plan surface, but no track owns a receipt-schema minor-versioning procedure. Assign to Track C (extend C3-2) before A7.3 lands in Phase 3.
- GAP — B6.4/C2-4 promotion clocks: 'two consecutive green release-check runs' and '≥7-day gap-free SLO window' are calendar requirements, not work items; the schedule must hold Phase 4/5 slack for them or promotions silently slip past the version tag.
- GAP — stale tracker hygiene beyond Block G: C13/C14/C15 stale 'pending' markers and the outdated D05 'remaining' paragraph (SDKs now published per recent commits) need a small cleanup task; fold into C1-3 acceptance.
- GAP — LongMemEval single-session-assistant and knowledge-update splits: per-type targets exist for preference/multi-session/temporal/knowledge-update but no task addresses single-session-assistant; acceptable (already high) but the F3.1 baseline must still gate it against regression.

### B.2 Состязательная критика (поправки обязательны)

**Вердикт.** The plan's factual grounding is excellent — every spot-checked claim (fusion scale hack, cosine fix landed, top_k.max(64) cap, b8 at 81.07% recall with answers.jsonl already generated but unjudged, blind feedback allocation, O_EXCL lock, 193M+148?? uncommitted accountability tree, zero tracker mentions) verified true in-repo. But as an executable program it has five structural defects. (1) OPUS-EXECUTABILITY: ~60% of tasks are genuinely cold-executable (A1.1, A1.4, B1.1, D1.1, D3.1, F2.2, TE2.1 are model specs with exact files/lines/acceptance). Worst offenders: C1-1 demands a phase-ordered, individually-compiling split of 341 files where shared files (retrieval_rank.rs, cell_ids.rs, Cargo.toml) carry interleaved multi-pillar hunks — under-specified AND under-sized; the correct goal is 'land safely in few coarse commits + clean-clone CI', not commit archaeology. C2-1/C2-2/C2-3 are procurement tasks (HSM custody, external compliance reviewer, 2 independent witnesses + 7-day SLO window) an agent cannot perform — split agent deliverables (runbook, dry-run) from human acquisition. A5.2's trainer is hand-waved ('coordinate-descent or grid over Q16') with a likely-unsatisfiable 'zero heldout row regressions' acceptance. B4.4's plan/commit protocol leaves group_id lifetime and payload-size-vs-2MB-cap unspecified. Several A6/B8 tasks make LLM-judged numeric outcomes the gate acceptance — an agent cannot guarantee GPT-4o's score, and target-as-gate incentivizes the exact benchmark-specific overfitting the plan forbids; gates must assert evidence+no-regression, targets go in narrative. (2) BENCHMARK REALISM: preference 0.2667→0.55 is REALISTIC, likely conservative — the type-aware prompt alone took DeepSeek preference 0/30→20/30; it's a harness fix (A6.2), which means the two duplicate deterministic preference-extraction pipelines (A4.3 and B3.1-B3.3) are NOT needed to hit the target. ERB 47.74→≥60 is REALISTIC via A6.1 (b8 answers exist, only judging remains — days of work). The fantasy-adjacent parts: 'engine path within 2 points of bench pipeline' (A7.2/A6.4) is a quarter-scale port with zero latency budget stated (full-payload scoring of 512 candidates conflicts with D4 p99 goals); multi-session 0.6391→0.75 assumes +2-3 retrieval recall points convert to +11 QA points — call it 0.70-0.75 stretch. Missing levers: no embedding-model selection/eval task anywhere (A2 builds plumbing; semantic 28.8→60 depends on WHICH model, never chosen); no 'judge of record' decision (F2.2's same-judge guard makes A6.4's ≥60 ill-defined — 60 on gemini-flash or gpt-5.4?); no cheap shared per-PR eval loop — Track F's regression baselines (F1/F3) are sequenced parallel to Track A when they must precede it. (3) DOUBLE-WORK: cosine ownership is handled correctly; but preference extraction is fully duplicated (A4.3 vs B3.1/B3.2 — same new file memory/preference_extract.rs, same lexicon, same pack slot, both claim gate 'preference-extract-check'); temporal windows duplicated (A4.1 vs B2.2 — same Date-anchor→Q16-boost feature, two specs, two gates); the pgvector+OPA baseline is built twice (TE4.2 XL in baselines/ vs F4.3 in scripts/aab/adapters/, both citing BASE-1/BASE-2); the six-axis AAB scorer has three claimed owners (C 'v0.5 harness', E 'consumes runners', F F4.2 builds it); /v1/auth/whoami built in both D2.2 and TE2.1 with different response shapes; five tasks across A and B edit the same lines of scripts/longmemeval/v1_cortexdb_retrieval.py; gate name 'ann-sparse-scope-recall-check' is defined by A3.2 AND already exists in Track C's fail-closed family. Also every A/B ranking task adds constants to frozen_weights.rs but omits C3-1's mandatory determinism-hash re-baseline from its acceptance — an agent doing A1.1 cold will break pack-determinism-hash-check and not know why. (4) DETERMINISM/HONESTY BREAKS: A3.3's persisted recall ring-buffer makes serving strategy depend on query-arrival history — identical query, different result set, no plan-visible cause; unless window/degradation state is bound into the signed receipt this breaks the 'verifier re-executes the plan' moat, and the task never mentions receipts. A4.3 says preference extraction 'default ON for MCP remember' while B3.1 says default false — default-ON implicit writes on REMEMBER violate least-surprise/fail-closed culture. B2.3's SUPERSEDES patching the TARGET cell's metadata lets one agent rewrite another cell's header — needs policy gating and an audit/receipt story, and rubs against the conflict-preservation-not-LWW positioning. Numeric-target gates (A6.2/A6.4/B8.2) pressure toward benchmark-specific defaults. (5) OVER-ENGINEERED vs MISSING: over-engineered — D3.2-D3.6 (2 XL + 2 L building a self-driving Raft cluster whose claim stays frozen regardless per F02); TE3.2's THREE independent receipt-verifier crypto implementations (Rust+Python-native+TS-native) create three canonicalization drift surfaces, defeating C4-2's spec-normative goal — wrap the wasm/binary; TE1.5 MCP Streamable-HTTP, TE2.8 pixel-diff regression, B7.2 usage accounting, B5.2 graph projection, F4.4 signed leaderboard pack, A8.3, D4.5 — all deferrable. Missing — a TODAY backup of the uncommitted tree (git branch + bundle, 5 minutes, cheapest existential insurance, in no task); a repo-wide merge freeze until C1-1 lands (stated only inside Track C's dependencies, absent from other tracks' depends_on); embedding model evaluation; latency acceptance for A7.2; an MVP definition across ~142 tasks (roughly 2-4 engineer-years with no cross-track prioritization). (6) EFFORT MIS-SIZING: C1-1 L→XL (or re-scope to M as coarse landing); A2.2 L→XL (checkpoint/HNSW/failure-mode interactions); A7.2 L→XL; A6.1 M→S (make targets over an existing artifact); TE4.2 XL→L once merged with F4.3; Track F is the best-sized track. NET: the plan is 80% right about WHAT and 40% right about WHO/WHEN — fix ownership collisions, re-sequence evidence infrastructure ahead of ranking changes, and land the uncommitted moat before anything else touches the tree.

| Фаза | Тег | Срок | Тема | Milestone-exit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Phase 0 — Preserve the moat** | v0.2.0-beta.3 | 3-7 days | Land the uncommitted accountability tree; nothing else merges first | git status clean; make alpha-check and release-check green in CI from a fresh clone |
| **Phase 1 — Cheap evidence wins + measurement backbone** | evidence-v1 | 1-2 weeks | Convert built-but-unscored artifacts into official numbers; regression gates before ranking changes | Registry live; ERB and LongMemEval headline improvements recorded with honest caveats; per-type regression gates green in nightly |
| **Phase 2 — Correctness and safety fixes** | v0.3.0-beta.1 | 2-4 weeks | Verified defects closed under the frozen-weights protocol | retrieval-fusion-sanity, feedback-allocation, lock-liveness, raft-hardstate gates green; rerank_mode=weighted provably ≥ none on ERB retrieval-only |
| **Phase 3 — Embeddings and the engine product path** | v0.3.0 | 4-8 weeks | Text-in/context-out RAG through the governed path; merged memory epics | ERB engine-path run recorded (target ≥60 same-judge, measured honestly either way); LongMemEval hybrid recall ≥0.93; zero external vector files |
| **Phase 4 — Production single-node + adoption surfaces** | v1.0.0 | 6-10 weeks, parallel lanes | production-v1-check and the external-facing moat | production-v1-check green with the conditional public claim; MCP v2 + receipt-visible console + 100% SDK parity matrix; AAB-mini verdict=RANKED |
| **Phase 5 — Stretch and ecosystem** | v1.1+ | open-ended | Learned ranker, consolidation, remaining adoption polish | Honest Mem0/Zep/Letta comparison publishable from gate evidence; multi-session ≥0.72 measured |

**Phase 0 — Preserve the moat — состав:**
- Immediate branch commit + git bundle backup of the 193M+148?? tree
- C1-1 re-scoped to coarse reviewed commits (not per-pillar splits)
- C1-2 clean-clone CI green for all accountability gates
- C1-3 tracker Block G + stale-claims sweep
- Repo-wide merge freeze communicated to all tracks

**Phase 1 — Cheap evidence wins + measurement backbone — состав:**
- A6.1 judge the b8 artifact (expect ~60-68 ERB combined)
- A6.2 type-aware official LongMemEval generation (expect preference ≥0.55, overall ~0.78-0.81)
- Judge-of-record decision + F2.1 rejudge target (blocked-status honest)
- F1.1/F1.2 benchmark registry seeded with 4 official results
- F2.2 same-judge comparator; F3.1/F3.2 per-type regression baselines
- F5.1 generated results page

**Phase 2 — Correctness and safety fixes — состав:**
- A1.1 fusion normalization + A1.2 corpus stats + A1.3 metric sweep + A1.4 pool depth (acceptance includes determinism-hash re-baseline per C3-1)
- B1.1 feedback allocator + B1.2 slot bound + B1.3 idempotency ledger
- D1.1 flock + D1.2 segment GC + D1.3 PITR defaults + D1.4 production profile
- D3.1 Raft hard-state (rest of D3 deferred)
- C3-1/C3-2 interface contracts frozen

**Phase 3 — Embeddings and the engine product path — состав:**
- NEW: embedding model selection eval on ERB 50-slice
- A2.1-A2.3 adapter/ingest/query embedding
- A3.1 HNSW graph-descent insert; A3.2 sparse-scope policy (A3.3 sampling only after receipt design review)
- Merged temporal epic (A4.1+B2.2) and supersession epic (B2.1/B2.3 with policy gate)
- Merged preference epic only if A6.2 leaves headroom

**Phase 4 — Production single-node + adoption surfaces — состав:**
- D2 IAM (RBAC v2, whoami, JWT+JWKS)
- D4.1-D4.4 tiered storage + p99 budgets; D5 observability/deployment; D6 format freeze + production-v1-check
- TE1 MCP tools (stdio only) + TE2.1/TE2.2/TE2.3 console core + TE3.1/3.3/3.4 SDK parity
- F4.1/F4.2 AAB-mini + merged pgvector baseline (F4.3); TE4.1 GCE spec v1.0; TE6.1 quickstart
- C2 evidence procurement in background (production_ready=false standing)

**Phase 5 — Stretch and ecosystem — состав:**
- A5 learned Q16 ranker (after enough real traces exist)
- B4 consolidation loop + B5 session linking + B6 transactions API
- TE5 framework integrations; TE4.2-4.4 AAB publication; deferred cuts revisited (MCP HTTP, visual regression, D3 cluster driver if F02 unfreezes)

**Критический путь:**
1. DO FIRST 1 — TODAY, before any agent edits the repo: snapshot the uncommitted accountability tree (git checkout -b accountability-landing && git add -A && git commit; plus git bundle to a second disk). Then C1-1 re-scoped as a coarse landing (2-4 reviewed commits, not per-pillar archaeology) + C1-2 clean-clone CI green + C1-3 tracker Block G. Institute an explicit merge freeze for all other tracks until this lands — currently that rule lives only inside Track C's dependency notes.
2. DO FIRST 2 — Cheap headline wins with zero engine risk: A6.1 judge the existing b8 artifact (answers.jsonl already generated at 81.07% recall; only the judge run + oracle audit remain — days, expected ~+14 ERB points) and A6.2 port the proven type-aware LongMemEval prompt to official GPT-4o generation (preference 0.2667→~0.55-0.65 from a prompt file). Decide the 'judge of record' (gemini-flash interim vs gpt-5.4 official) before publishing either number.
3. DO FIRST 3 — Re-sequence Track F ahead of Track A: F1.1/F1.2 evidence registry seeded with the 4 existing official results, F2.2 same-judge comparator, F3.1/F3.2 per-type regression baselines. No ranking change (A1.x onward) merges until the regression gates that would catch its regressions exist.
4. DO FIRST 4 — A1.1 fusion normalization + A1.4 candidate-pool depth (verified real defects: recency_q16*1024 scale hack, top_k.max(64) cap), landed THROUGH C3-1's frozen-weights protocol with the determinism-hash golden re-baseline written into A1.1's acceptance criteria (currently missing).
5. DO FIRST 5 — The three small verified safety holes: B1.1 feedback silent-overwrite fix (blind current_seq+1 confirmed at feedback.rs:83-92), D1.1 flock lock (O_EXCL confirmed at lock.rs:20), D3.1 Raft hard-state persistence (cheap Raft-safety fix worth doing even with D3.2+ deferred).
6. THEN — merged single-owner epics: ONE temporal-window epic (A4.1+B2.2, Track B owns retrieve semantics), ONE preference epic (A4.3+B3.1/B3.2, only if the prompt fix leaves headroom below 0.55), ONE supersession epic (B2.1/B2.3 with a policy gate on cross-cell metadata patching), embeddings A2.1→A2.2→A2.3 preceded by a new embedding-model-selection task, then A7.1/A7.2 with an explicit latency budget, closing with A6.4 engine-path run.
7. PARALLEL AFTER LANDING — D1.2-D1.4 + D2 IAM + D6 format freeze toward production-v1-check; TE2.1 dashboard auth (consuming D2.2's whoami — delete TE2.1's duplicate endpoint spec); TE1 MCP tools minus HTTP transport; F4.2 AAB-mini with the pgvector baseline merged into a single Track-F-owned implementation (F4.3 design wins; TE4.2 folds in).
8. HUMAN-DEPENDENT TRACK (never blocks releases) — C2-1/C2-2/C2-3 production evidence procurement stays production_ready=false as plan of record; agent deliverable is runbooks + dry-runs only.

**Параллельные дорожки:**
- After Phase 0 landing, four genuinely independent lanes exist: (1) Evidence/benchmarks (Track F + A6.1/A6.2) — touches scripts/ and fixtures/ only; (2) Engine ranking (A1→A2→A7 + merged temporal/preference/supersession) — single-writer on retrieval_rank.rs/frozen_weights.rs, serialized internally, all changes through C3-1 frozen-weights protocol; (3) Ops hardening (D1, D2, D4.1-D4.4, D6) — touches lock/lifecycle/auth/storage, near-zero overlap with lane 2; (4) Interfaces (TE1 minus HTTP, TE2 after D2.2 whoami, TE3.1/3.3/3.4) — touches cortex-mcp, web/dashboard, sdk/.
- NOT parallelizable despite the plan's implication: Track A and Track B ranking/memory work (both edit retrieval_rank.rs, frozen_weights.rs, exec/retrieve.rs, and the same lines of scripts/longmemeval/v1_cortexdb_retrieval.py) — merge into one serialized engine lane; Track F baselines and Track A ranking changes (F must land its regression gates first, then A re-baselines through the registry).
- Human-clock-bound and fully decoupled: C2 production-evidence procurement (KMS/HSM, compliance reviewer, external witnesses, 7-day SLO windows) runs on vendor/business timelines — keep it off every release critical path with production_ready=false as the standing honest state.

**Топ-риски:**
| Риск | Митигейт |
|---|---|
| The entire accountability implementation (193 modified + 148 untracked files, verified) exists only in one working tree on one machine; any of the ~140 planned tasks starting with a checkout/reset destroys the category moat, and the landing-precedence rule is stated only inside Track C's prose. | Phase 0 hard gate: branch-commit + git bundle backup today; coarse-commit landing within a week; explicit merge freeze added to every track's depends_on; clean-clone CI as the exit proof. |
| Cross-track file collisions guarantee destructive merges: retrieval_rank.rs is edited by A1.1/A1.2/A4.1/A5.2/B2.2 and carries uncommitted Track C changes; v1_cortexdb_retrieval.py is edited by 5 tasks across A and B; frozen_weights.rs gains constants from 4 tasks; two tracks define the same gate names. | Single-writer-per-file ownership map; merge the duplicate temporal/preference/pgvector/whoami epics before assignment; add C3-1's determinism-hash re-baseline to every ranking task's acceptance criteria. |
| Benchmark targets encoded as gate thresholds (LongMemEval ≥0.80, ERB ≥60, knowledge-update ≥0.92) with LLM judges in the loop pressure agents toward benchmark-specific defaults — violating the plan's own no-overfit and PUBLIC_CLAIMS honesty rules — and the ERB target is undefined until a judge-of-record is chosen (F2.2 forbids cross-judge comparison). | Gates assert evidence-exists + no-regression-vs-committed-baseline only; numeric targets live in exit-criteria narrative with measured-value honesty; decide judge-of-record in Phase 1 before publishing any new headline. |
| Two targets are fantasy-adjacent as floors: multi-session QA 0.6391→0.75 (retrieval deltas of +2-3 points don't mechanically yield +11 QA points) and 'engine path within 2 points of the b8 bench pipeline' (a quarter-scale port with no latency budget, conflicting with D4 p99 goals). | Reclassify both as stretch (0.70-0.75 range; 'within 5 points' initial parity); add a latency acceptance to A7.2; never block a release on either. |
| A3.3's persisted recall-window sampling makes ANN serving depend on query-arrival history with no receipt visibility — silently breaking the 'verifier re-executes the signed plan' moat property; similarly B2.3 SUPERSEDES lets one agent patch another cell's metadata with no policy/audit story. | Mandatory Track C design review before A3.3 (degradation state must be receipt-visible or per-collection-epoch); B2.3 gains a policy check (same-agent or explicit capability) and audit-chain entry for target-cell patches. |
| Track C Phase-9 production evidence (HSM custody, external compliance certification, ≥2 independent witnesses over a 7-day gap-free window) requires vendors, contracts, and calendar time no agent controls; treating it as plan tasks creates permanent red blockers. | Reclassify C2-1..C2-3 as human procurement with agent-deliverable runbooks/dry-runs; production_ready=false with 'signed receipt (beta, local trust anchor)' is the standing honest claim and never blocks v1.0. |
| Capacity: ~142 tasks (26 A, 28 B, 14 C, 27 D, 30 E, 17 F) with many L/XL is roughly 2-4 engineer-years; with no cross-track MVP definition the plan defaults to everything-in-parallel and the moat-critical work starves. | Adopt the 6-phase sequencing above; execute the 3 cuts (D3 driver program, duplicate pipelines, Track E gold-plating) immediately — they remove ~15 tasks including 4 XL/L items without touching any exit-critical claim. |

**Срезы и пробелы:**
- CUT 1 — D3.2-D3.6 Raft driver/cluster CLI/failover SLO/consensus metrics (~2 XL + 2 L + 1 M): the F02 freeze means the HA claim cannot be published regardless of how well it works, and the project's own unfreeze rule requires external demand that does not exist. Keep only D3.1 (hard-state persistence, real Raft safety bug). Revisit when ≥3 external HA requests materialize.
- CUT 2 — All duplicate pipelines, consolidated to one owner: merge A4.3+B3.1/B3.2 (preference — and gate it on whether A6.2's prompt fix leaves headroom), merge A4.1+B2.2 (temporal windows), merge TE4.2 into F4.3 (one pgvector+OPA baseline, Track F owns, snapshot-offline design), D2.2 owns whoami (TE2.1 consumes), one owner per shared file (retrieval_rank.rs, frozen_weights.rs, v1_cortexdb_retrieval.py get a single-writer rule). Saves ~5 redundant tasks and eliminates 4 gate-name/report-path collisions including the duplicated preference-extract-check and ann-sparse-scope-recall-check.
- CUT 3 — Track E gold-plating: TE3.2 native Python/TS receipt-verifier reimplementations (three crypto implementations = three canonicalization drift surfaces; wrap the wasm build / shell out to the binary instead), TE1.5 MCP Streamable-HTTP transport (stdio covers current MCP clients), TE2.8's pixel-diff visual regression (keep the functional Playwright conversion). Also defer: B7.2 usage accounting, B5.2 session graph projection, F4.4 signed leaderboard pack, A8.3 adapter conformance, D4.5 10M packet, TE5.2 llamaindex.
- GAP 1 — No task backs up the uncommitted 341-file tree before work begins, and the 'C1-1 precedes all merges' rule appears only in Track C's prose — absent from every other track's depends_on. One git checkout . by any of the ~140 planned agent tasks destroys the moat. Phase 0 must be a hard gate on all tracks.
- GAP 2 — No embedding-model selection/evaluation task: A2 builds adapter plumbing but the ERB semantic 28.8→60 and LongMemEval hybrid targets depend primarily on which model/dimension is chosen and what it costs at ingest. Add a task: evaluate 2-3 candidate models on the ERB 50-question slice before A2.2 hardcodes a profile.
- GAP 3 — No cheap per-PR retrieval eval loop: every A/B ranking task invents its own 50-question A/B procedure. One shared harness (cached corpora, per-type output, sub-5-minute) should be Track F's first deliverable and every ranking task's mandatory gate — eval-driven development is claimed in spirit but not wired in sequence.
- GAP 4 — A7.2 two-stage engine retrieve has no latency acceptance criterion despite scoring full payloads for 512 candidates per query — directly at odds with D4.4's p99 budgets. Add a perf budget to its acceptance or the benchmark win becomes a production regression.
- GAP 5 — Numeric benchmark targets are used as gate acceptance (A6.2 ≥0.80, A6.4 ≥60, B8.2 ≥0.92): an agent cannot guarantee an LLM judge's output, and target-as-gate pressures toward the benchmark-specific defaults the plan itself forbids. Gates should assert evidence-exists + no-regression; targets move to exit-criteria narrative with measured-value honesty.
- GAP 6 — A3.3's persisted recall window makes ANN serving depend on query-arrival history with no receipt visibility: needs a joint design review with Track C before implementation (degradation state must be plan/receipt-visible) or it silently breaks the verifier-re-executes moat property.
- GAP 7 — No judge-of-record decision for ERB: F2.2 correctly forbids cross-judge deltas, which makes A6.4's '≥60' undefined until someone decides whether the exit number is gemini-flash-interim or gpt-5.4-official.

## Приложение C. Реестр гейтов (`make <name>`)

Сводный список гейтов, упомянутых в задачах плана (конвенция проекта: каждый пишет `target/<gate>/report.json`). Новые гейты создаются задачами, существующие — переиспользуются:
